judging me, even when they swear they aren’t.”\n▸ Patient-specific Symptom Manifestations\n- S019 (Delusions): At the shop and even at church, I get convinced people are whispering about me or setting me up to fail—like when my supervisor moved me off a big EV job, I was sure he and my husband had talked and decided I shouldn’t be trusted around certain cars, and no matter how many times they both tell me that’s not true, I can’t shake the feeling there’s this plan going on behind my back.\n- S020 (Hallucinations): Every so often, especially late at night in the garage or when I’m alone in the house, I hear this man’s voice clear as day commenting on what I’m doing—saying things like, “They’re laughing at you,” or “You’re wasting your time,” even though I know there’s nobody actually there talking to me.\n- S023 (Negative_Symptoms): Compared to how I used to be—organizing meets for the car club and always messing with some project—now on a lot of days I just sit on the couch after work staring at the TV, not really caring about hanging out with friends or even finishing the old Camaro in the driveway, and my wife has to push me just to shower or answer texts back.\n- S033 (Elevated_Irritable_Mood): My mood has been all over the place—some days I’m on top of the world and talking a mile a minute about baby names and new ideas for the shop, and then out of nowhere the smallest comment from my wife or a coworker will set me off and I’ll snap at them like they’re attacking me, and only later do I realize I overreacted.\n- S034 (Increased_Activity): When I’m in one of those up phases, I can’t sit still at all—I’ll stay at the garage after my shift ends, start three different repair projects, reorganize all my tools, and then come home and decide to deep-clean the kitchen at one in the morning because I feel this constant push to keep doing something.\n- S035 (Inflated_Self_Esteem): I get these stretches where I honestly believe I’m the only one at the shop who really understands the new EV systems and that I’m meant to change how the whole place runs, like management should just hand me the training program and let me run it because I “see the bigger picture” that everyone else is too small-minded to get.\n- S036 (Decreased_Sleep): There have been weeks where I’ll sleep maybe three or four hours a night, lying there wide awake with my mind racing about work and the baby we might have one day, but I still jump out of bed feeling wired and tell my wife I don’t really need sleep because I’ve got too much to do anyway.\n- S037 (Pressured_Speech): My wife and my coworkers keep telling me that lately, when I get going about a new project or some idea I have about people watching me, I talk so fast and jump from one thing to another that they can’t get a word in, and even when they try to interrupt I feel this pressure in my chest to keep talking until I’ve gotten it all out.\n▸ Additional Diagnostic Requirements\n- psychotic_symptoms_and_manic_episode_occur_concurrently: When I first started staying late at the shop full of energy, barely sleeping and taking on extra EV projects, that was also when the voice really kicked in and I became convinced my coworkers and my wife were working together to undermine me.\n- manic_episode_is_present_for_the_majority_of_the_total_duration_of_the_active_and_residual_portions_of_the_illness: Looking back over this past year or so, most of the time I’ve either been in those wired, overconfident phases where I’m nonstop busy and talking fast, or just coming down from them, with only short stretches where my mood feels more normal.\n- delusions_and_hallucinations_occur_for_2_or_more_weeks_in_the_absence_of_a_major_mood_episode: There was a stretch this past spring when my mood had mostly flattened out and I wasn’t especially up or down, but for a few weeks I still kept hearing that man’s voice and was sure people at work were plotting against me even though I wasn’t in one of my high-energy phases.\n- functional_impairment: Because of all this, my supervisor cut back my responsibilities after I argued with him about being sabotaged, my wife and I barely talk without fighting, and I’ve stopped going to most car‑club events or keeping up with basic things at home like bills and cleaning.\n- duration: All of this really started to build up about a year and a few months ago when we began seriously planning for a baby and I took on the extra EV training work at the shop.\n- psychotic_symptoms_duration: The voice and the strong feeling that people are plotting against me have been hanging around most of the time since late last fall.\n- manic_episode_duration: The periods where I’m revved up, sleeping very little, and throwing myself into project after project have been coming and going for close to a kitchen at one in the morning because I feel this constant push to keep doing something.\n- S035 (Inflated_Self_Esteem): I get these stretches where I honestly believe I’m the only one at the shop who really understands the new EV systems and that I’m meant to change how the whole place runs, like management should just hand me the training program and let me run it because I “see the bigger picture” that everyone else is too small-minded to get.\n- S036 (Decreased_Sleep): There have been weeks where I’ll sleep maybe three or four hours a night, lying there wide awake with my mind racing about work and the baby we might have one day, but I still jump out of bed feeling wired and tell my wife I don’t really need sleep because I’ve got too much to do anyway.\n- S037 (Pressured_Speech): can’t get a word in, and even when they try to interrupt I feel this pressure in my chest to keep talking until I’ve gotten it all out.\n▸ Additional Diagnostic Requirements\n- wired, overconfident phases where I’m nonstop busy and talking fast, or just coming down from them, with only short stretches where my mood feels more normal.\n- delusions_and_hallucinations_occur_for_2_or_more_weeks_in_the_absence_of_a_major_mood_episode: There was a stretch this past spring when my mood had mostly flattened out and I wasn’t especially up or talking to me.\n- S023 (Negative_Symptoms): Compared to how I used to be—organizing meets for the car club and always messing with some project—now on a lot of days I just sit on the couch after work staring at the TV, not really caring about hanging out with friends or even finishing the old Camaro in the driveway, and my wife has to push me just to shower or answer texts back.\n- S033 (Elevated_Irritable_Mood): My mood has been all over the place—some days I’m on top of the world and talking a mile a minute about baby names and new ideas for the shop, and then out of nowhere the smallest comment from my wife or a coworker will set me off and I’ll snap at them like they’re attacking me, and only later do I realize I overreacted.\n- S034 (Increased_Activity): When I’m in one of those up phases, I can’t sit still at all—I’ll stay at the garage after my shift ends, start three different repair projects, reorganize all my tools, and then come home and decide to deep-clean the kitchen at one in the morning because I feel this constant push to keep doing something.\n- S035 (Inflated_Self_Esteem): I get these stretches where I honestly believe I’m the only one at the shop who really understands the new EV systems and that I’m meant to change how the whole place runs, like management should just hand me the training program and let me run it because I “see the bigger picture” that everyone else is too small-minded to get.\n- S036 (Decreased_Sleep): There have been weeks where I’ll sleep maybe three or four hours a night, lying there wide awake with my mind racing about work and the baby we might have one day, but I still jump out of bed feeling wired and tell my wife I don’t really need sleep because I’ve got too much to do anyway.\n- S037 (Pressured_Speech): My wife and my coworkers keep telling me that lately, when I get going about a new project or some idea I have about people watching me, I talk so fast and jump from one thing to another that they can’t get a word in, and even when they try to interrupt I feel this pressure in my chest to keep talking until I’ve gotten it all out.\n▸ Additional Diagnostic Requirements\n- psychotic_symptoms_and_manic_episode_occur_concurrently: When I first started staying late at the shop full of energy, barely sleeping and taking on extra EV projects, that was also when the voice wired, overconfident phases where I’m nonstop busy and talking fast, or just coming down from them, with only short stretches where my mood feels more normal.\n- delusions_and_hallucinations_occur_for_2_or_more_weeks_in_the_absence_of_a_major_mood_episode: There was a stretch this past spring when my mood had mostly flattened out and I wasn’t especially up or down, but for a few weeks I still kept hearing that man’s voice and was sure people at work were plotting against me even though I wasn’t in one of my high-energy phases.\n- functional_impairment: Because of all this, my supervisor cut back my responsibilities after I argued with him about being sabotaged, my wife and I barely talk without fighting, and I’ve stopped going to most car‑club But I don't feel like anyone is controlling my thoughts.\n\nDoctor: Have these beliefs and the voice been present continuously for more than one month, or did they come on suddenly and change a lot over a short period of days or weeks?\n\nPatient: They've definitely been there for much longer than a month. They didn't just appear out of nowhere and change over a few days; they've been hanging around pretty consistently since late last fall.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever had a period of at least a few days where your mood was very high or very irritable, you needed much less sleep, or your thoughts raced unusually fast?\n\nPatient: Yes, I've had periods like that coming and going for close to a year now. I'll get so wired that I only sleep three or four hours a night with my mind racing about work and baby plans, and my mood swings from feeling on top of the world to snapping at my wife or coworkers over minor things.\n\nDoctor: Have you had a stretch of at least 2 weeks when the hallucinations or paranoid beliefs continued even when your mood was not unusually high, irritable, or depressed?\n\nPatient: Yes, that actually happened this past spring. My mood had mostly flattened out and I wasn't especially up or down, but for a few weeks I still kept hearing that man's voice and felt sure people at work were plotting against me.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever had at least 2 weeks when you were hearing the voice or feeling paranoid while your mood was completely normal and you were not having any manic or depressive symptoms?\n\nPatient: Yes, that's what happened this past spring. My mood had mostly flattened out and I wasn't feeling up or down, but for a few weeks I still kept hearing that man's voice and felt convinced people at work were plotting against me.\n\nDoctor: Have you also had periods of depression, like feeling very down, losing interest, or having low energy, that lasted for at least 2 weeks?\n\nPatient: I do get these phases where I lose all my drive and interest, though I'm not sure if it's official depression. On those days, I just sit on the couch staring at the TV, neglecting my car projects, and my wife has to push me just to shower or answer texts. It's like a major loss of energy and motivation that can last for weeks.\n\nDoctor: Have those low mood or loss-of-interest periods lasted for at least 2 weeks at a time, with symptoms like feeling down, losing pleasure, low energy, or trouble concentrating?\n\nPatient: I haven't really experienced prolonged periods of feeling deeply down or depressed like that. I definitely have those days where I completely lose interest and just sit on the couch, but I wouldn't say I feel deeply sad or depressed for weeks at a time.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever had a clear manic episode that lasted at least 1 week, or a major depressive episode that lasted at least 2 weeks, with the hallucinations or paranoia happening only during those mood episodes?\n\nPatient: No, they didn't happen only during those high-energy phases. Like I mentioned, this past spring when my mood had flattened out and I felt pretty normal, I still heard the voice and felt like people were plotting against me for a few weeks."

They didn't just appear out of nowhere and change over a few days; they've been hanging around pretty consistently since late last fall.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever had a period of at least a few days where your mood was very high or very irritable, you needed much less sleep, or your thoughts raced unusually fast?\n\nPatient: Yes, I've had periods like that coming and going for close to a year now. I'll get so wired that I only sleep three or four hours a night with my mind racing about work and baby plans, and my mood swings from feeling on top of the world to snapping at my wife or coworkers over minor things.\n\nDoctor: Have you had a stretch of at least 2 weeks when the hallucinations or paranoid beliefs continued even when your mood was not unusually high, irritable, or depressed?\n\nPatient: Yes, that actually happened this past spring. My mood had mostly flattened out and I wasn't especially up or down, but for a few weeks I still kept hearing that man's voice and felt sure people at work were plotting against me.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever had at least 2 weeks when you were hearing the voice or feeling paranoid while your mood was completely normal and you were not having any manic or depressive symptoms?\n\nPatient: Yes, that's what happened this past spring. My mood had mostly flattened out and I wasn't feeling up or down, but for a few weeks I still kept hearing that man's voice and felt convinced people at work were plotting against me.\n\nDoctor: Have you also had periods of depression, like feeling very down, losing interest, or having low energy, that lasted for at least 2 weeks?\n\nPatient: I do get these phases where I lose all my drive and interest, though I'm not sure if it's official depression. On those days, I just sit on the couch staring at the TV, neglecting my car projects, and my wife has to push me just to shower or answer texts. It's like a major loss of energy and motivation that can last for weeks.\n\nDoctor: Have those low mood or loss-of-interest periods lasted for at least 2 weeks at a time, with symptoms like feeling down, losing pleasure, low energy, or trouble concentrating?\n\nPatient: I haven't really experienced prolonged periods of feeling deeply down or depressed like that. I definitely have those days where I completely lose interest and just sit on the couch, but I wouldn't say I feel deeply sad or depressed for weeks at a time.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever had a clear manic episode that lasted at least 1 week, or a major depressive episode that lasted at least 2 weeks, with the hallucinations or paranoia happening only during those mood episodes?\n\nPatient: No, they didn't happen only during those high-energy phases. Like I mentioned, this past spring when my mood had flattened out and I felt pretty normal, I still heard the voice and felt like people were plotting against me for a few weeks.\n\nDoctor: Have these mood episodes and the hallucinations/paranoid beliefs been present for at least 6 months overall, with ongoing problems in work, relationships, or self-care during that time?\n\nPatient: Yes, this has been going on for over a year now, and it has seriously disrupted my life. My supervisor cut my responsibilities at the shop after we argued, my wife and I fight constantly, and I've stopped going to my car club. I also struggle with basic things at home like bills, cleaning, and even showering unless my wife pushes me to do it."

I also hear a man's voice every so often, especially when I'm alone or working late in the garage, commenting on what I'm doing. But I don't feel like anyone is controlling my thoughts.\n\nDoctor: Have these beliefs and the voice been present continuously for more than one month, or did they come on suddenly and change a lot over a short period of days or weeks?\n\nPatient: They've definitely been there for much longer than a month. They didn't just appear out of nowhere and change over a few days; they've been hanging around pretty consistently since late last fall.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever had a period of at least a few days where your mood was very high or very irritable, you needed much less sleep, or your thoughts raced unusually fast?\n\nPatient: Yes, I've had periods like that coming and going for close to a year now. I'll get so wired that I only sleep three or four hours a night with my mind racing about work and baby plans, and my mood swings from feeling on top of the world to snapping at my wife or coworkers over minor things.\n\nDoctor: Have you had a stretch of at least 2 weeks when the hallucinations or paranoid beliefs continued even when your mood was not unusually high, irritable, or depressed?\n\nPatient: Yes, that actually happened this past spring. My mood had mostly flattened out and I wasn't especially up or down, but for a few weeks I still kept hearing that man's voice and felt sure people at work were plotting against me.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever had at least 2 weeks when you were hearing the voice or feeling paranoid while your mood was completely normal and you were not having any manic or depressive symptoms?\n\nPatient: Yes, that's what happened this past spring. My mood had mostly flattened out and I wasn't feeling up or down, but for a few weeks I still kept hearing that man's voice and felt convinced people at work were plotting against me.\n\nDoctor: Have you also had periods of depression, like feeling very down, losing interest, or having low energy, that lasted for at least 2 weeks?\n\nPatient: I do get these phases where I lose all my drive and interest, though I'm not sure if it's official depression. On those days, I just sit on the couch staring at the TV, neglecting my car projects, and my wife has to push me just to shower or answer texts. It's like a major loss of energy and motivation that can last for weeks.\n\nDoctor: Have those low mood or loss-of-interest periods lasted for at least 2 weeks at a time, with symptoms like feeling down, losing pleasure, low energy, or trouble concentrating?\n\nPatient: I haven't really experienced prolonged periods of feeling deeply down or depressed like that. I definitely have those days where I completely lose interest and just sit on the couch, but I wouldn't say I feel deeply sad or depressed for weeks at a time.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever had a clear manic episode that lasted at least 1 week, or a major depressive episode that lasted at least 2 weeks, with the hallucinations or paranoia happening only during those mood episodes?\n\nPatient: No, they didn't happen only during those high-energy phases. Like I mentioned, this past spring when my mood had flattened out and I felt pretty normal, I still heard the voice and felt like people were plotting against me for a few weeks.\n\nDoctor: Have these mood episodes and the hallucinations/paranoid beliefs been present for at least 6 months overall, with ongoing problems in work, relationships, or self-care during that time?\n\nPatient: Yes, this has been going on for over a year now, and it has seriously disrupted my life. I'm also not aware of any family history of schizophrenia, bipolar disorder, or schizoaffective disorder."

Subtypes: Persistent Fear, Persistent Horror, Persistent Anger, Persistent Guilt, Persistent Shame\n[S075] Social_Detachment: Feelings of detachment or estrangement from others.
